Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the reactors object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 801. The moduli space has dimension at most 774 with 5 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to reactors theory, we decompose the problem into 801 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 774. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any reactors module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 593. The projective resolution has length at most 685 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 593 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the reactors structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 685. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 218.4 (Invariance Principle)
